public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {

public class TwoSum {
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {
        int n = nums.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                if (nums[i] + nums[j] == target) {
                    return new int[] {i, j};
                }
            }
        }
        return new int[0];
    }
}

public class TwoSum {
    //@ requires nums != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\exists int i, j; 0 < i < nums.length && i < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] == target) ==> \result.length == 2;  
    //@ ensures (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i < \result.length && 0 <= j < \result.length && i != j; \result[i] != \result[j]);
    //@ ensures (\result.length == 2 && nums[\result[0]] + nums[\result[1]] == target) || (\result.length == 0 && (\forall int i, j; 0 < i < nums.length && 0 < j < nums.length; nums[i] + nums[j] != target));  
    public static int[] twoSum(int[] nums, int target) {

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining \exists int k, l; 0 - 1 <= k <= i && k <= l <= n; nums[k] + nums[l] != target;  
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            //@ maintaining i + 1 <= j <= n;

                }

        return new int[0];
    }
